Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any probabilities module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 186. The projective resolution has length at most 767 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to probabilities theory, we decompose the problem into 186 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 767. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
